# Chart.js parity tracker

One row per upstream source file of [Chart.js v4.6.1](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/tree/v4.6.1),
the tag that ended the commit pin v4.5.1+7169e651 ([upstream-tracking.md](upstream-tracking.md) §2.4)
(the pinned `third_party/Chart.js` submodule; run `melos run upstream` to check it out).
Tool 6 ([porting-flow.md](porting-flow.md) §4) writes this file. Port work edits `doc/status.yaml` in the same PR as the code.

Status values ([porting-flow.md](porting-flow.md) §1 step 7):

- `not started`: no Dart code yet.
- `in progress`: Dart code exists, but not all of the module is ported.
- `partial: <symbols>`: only the listed symbols are ported, because a ported module needs them.
- `ported`: code + ported specs pass; for a pure-layer module, on the VM, dart2js and dart2wasm.
  This is the last status of a module. The port has no `verified` status
  ([Chart.js ADR 0034](adr/0034-no-display-only-js-exactness.md)).
- `replaced`: a Flutter file replaces the upstream file, for example `platform.flutter.dart` for
  `platform.dom.js`.
- `distributed`: a type declaration file mirrored as `abstract interface class` declarations in
  `lib/src/types`, with members added as ported callers need them (porting-flow.md §2.4).
- `n/a: <reason>`: not ported, for the stated reason.

Line counts are `wc -l` of the upstream file at the pin.

## Summary

The Ported column counts the rows with the status `ported`. It does not count `partial` rows. It
does not count `distributed` or `replaced` rows. The types area has 6 `distributed` rows. The
platform area has 1 `replaced` row. The last four rows count the [Upstream tests](#upstream-tests)
specs, the [test set-up](#test-set-up) file, the [@kurkle/color 0.3.2](#kurklecolor-032) files and
the [chartjs-test-utils 0.4.0](#chartjs-test-utils-040) files.

| Area | Files | Lines | Ported |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| helpers | 18 | 3437 | 18 |
| core | 21 | 7137 | 21 |
| elements | 5 | 1279 | 5 |
| scales | 8 | 2290 | 8 |
| controllers | 9 | 1948 | 9 |
| plugins | 15 | 3518 | 15 |
| platform | 4 | 508 | 3 |
| types | 7 | 4119 | 0 |
| entry points | 3 | 134 | 3 |
| upstream specs | 54 | 26375 | 54 |
| test set-up | 1 | 36 | 1 |
| @kurkle/color | 11 | 1126 | 10 |
| chartjs-test-utils | 7 | 950 | 5 |

Runtime dependency: [`@kurkle/color`](https://github.com/kurkle/color) `^0.3.0` (MIT, Jukka Kurkela), resolved to **0.3.2** in the `pnpm-lock.yaml` of the pin. It is wrapped by `src/helpers/helpers.color.ts`, parses and mixes CSS colours. The port of 0.3.2 is in `lib/src/kurkle_color/`. See [@kurkle/color 0.3.2](#kurklecolor-032).

## Core (`src/core`)

| Upstream file | Lines | Dart target | Status |
|---|---:|---|---|
| [`src/core/core.adapters.ts`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/core/core.adapters.ts) | 138 | `lib/src/core/core.adapters.dart` | ported (differences: 2 `adapt`, [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-date-adapter-prototype), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-date-default-adapter). No upstream spec. `DateAdapter`, `DateAdapterBase` and the default export `adapters`. `DateAdapterBase.jsClass` is the class value of `_date` ([Chart.js ADR 0020](adr/0020-registry-and-component-classes.md) §3). `DateAdapterBase.prototype` holds the members that `override` assigns. The members take and give JavaScript values. `DateAdapterBase.prototype` starts with the default members of the port-only `date_adapter.dart` ([Chart.js ADR 0024](adr/0024-dates.md)). A member of the exact Dart type of its method gets a direct call. The type `TimeUnit` is an extension type over `String` that implements `String`, with the 9 units of the union as constants and a public constructor for a custom unit ([Chart.js ADR 0052](adr/0052-typed-public-api.md) §7)) |
| [`src/core/core.animation.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/core/core.animation.js) | 119 | `lib/src/core/core.animation.dart` | ported (differences: 3 `adapt`, 1 `platform`, [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-animation-number-path), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-animation-color-cache), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-typed-elements), [differences.md](differences.md#platform-animation-promise-checkpoint); [Chart.js ADR 0019](adr/0019-animation.md): `Animation` and its three interpolators. A target is a Map or a `types.PropertyAccess`. The `@internal` functions `readProperty` and `writeProperty` read and write it by name. The constructor resolves the interpolator one time. A user `fn` gets a closure that calls it through `jsCall`. `Animation.typed` takes the `AnimatedProp` key of the typed table of `Animations`, and the number path writes an element through `Element.writeNumber` ([Chart.js ADR 0054](adr/0054-typed-internal-model.md) §10). `wait` returns the future of a `Completer.sync()`. The `_` members that the specs and the animator read drop the underscore and carry `@internal`. `Animation.jsClass` is the class as a value ([Chart.js ADR 0048](adr/0048-entry-points-and-public-library.md) §2). A pure layer) |

| [`src/core/core.animations.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/core/core.animations.js) | 162 | `lib/src/core/core.animations.dart` | ported (differences: 3 `adapt`, 1 `platform`, [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-animation-spec), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-typed-elements), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-typed-element-options), [differences.md](differences.md#platform-reduced-motion); [Chart.js ADR 0019](adr/0019-animation.md): `Animations` with `configure`, `update` and the private `_animateOptions`, `_createAnimations`, `_awaitAll` and `_resolveTargetOptions`. `configure` makes an `AnimationSpec` for each config entry. It reads the names of a resolver with `jsOwnPropertyNames` and each option through `readOption`. `_properties` is a table by `AnimatedProp` id with the key order of the JS `Map`, and a property object of a controller animates by its typed keys ([Chart.js ADR 0054](adr/0054-typed-internal-model.md) §10). `Promise.all` is `jsPromiseAll`. `_resolveTargetOptions` copies with `copyOptions`. `Animations.jsClass` is the class as a value ([Chart.js ADR 0048](adr/0048-entry-points-and-public-library.md) §2). `_createAnimations` has the reduced-motion hunk of [Chart.js ADR 0049](adr/0049-chart-view-widget.md) §7: for a canvas with the flag of `reduced_motion.dart`, it reads no animation config. Not a pure layer: it imports `core.animator`) |
| [`src/core/core.animator.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/core/core.animator.js) | 214 | `lib/src/core/core.animator.dart` | ported (differences: 3 `platform`, 1 `harness`, [differences.md](differences.md#platform-animation-promise-checkpoint), [differences.md](differences.md#platform-animator-mute), [differences.md](differences.md#platform-animator-skip-muted), [differences.md](differences.md#harness-method-fields); [Chart.js ADR 0019](adr/0019-animation.md) §8 and [Chart.js ADR 0016](adr/0016-frame-scheduling.md) decisions 6 and 7: the singleton `animator` on `requestAnimFrame`. Its frame callback runs inside `jsRunWithMicrotaskCheckpoint`. `update` walks the charts with `jsMapForEach`. `getAnims`, `update` and the field `refresh` drop the underscore for the fixtures, and `refresh` is a field that a fixture replaces ([Chart.js ADR 0029](adr/0029-fixture-smoke-runner.md) §6, row `harness-method-fields`). `AnimationEvent` is the event object of the listeners. `mute` and `unmute` serve the chart host. Not a pure layer: it imports `helpers.extras`) |
| [`src/core/core.config.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/core/core.config.js) | 418 | `lib/src/core/core.config.dart` | ported (differences: 3 `adapt`, 4 `theme`, [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-typed-options), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-typed-element-options), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-config-objects), [differences.md](differences.md#theme-H0), [differences.md](differences.md#theme-H1), [differences.md](differences.md#theme-H2), [differences.md](differences.md#theme-H3). No upstream spec. The hunks H0-H3 of [Chart.js ADR 0050](adr/0050-public-theme-api.md), the public H0 reader `chartThemeOf` of [Chart.js ADR 0050](adr/0050-public-theme-api.md) for hunk H5 of `plugin.colors`, and the parameter `into` of `resolveNamedOptions` ([Chart.js ADR 0021](adr/0021-typed-options-in-the-resolver.md) §1). `mergeScaleConfig` gives a typed `ScaleOptionsByIdScope` of `ScaleOptionsScope`s, and `resolveElementNamedOptions` is the typed element step ([Chart.js ADR 0054](adr/0054-typed-internal-model.md) §3-§4)) |
| [`src/core/core.controller.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/core/core.controller.js) | 1269 | `lib/src/core/core.controller.dart` | ported (differences: 5 `adapt`, 2 `platform`, 1 `harness`, [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-typed-plugins), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-component-class-role), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-active-elements-number-index), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-chart-registry-getter), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-chart-index-parameters), [differences.md](differences.md#platform-destroy-cancel-resize), [differences.md](differences.md#platform-to-base64-image), [differences.md](differences.md#harness-method-fields). The class `Chart` implements `types.Chart`. The statics follow [Chart.js ADR 0020](adr/0020-registry-and-component-classes.md) §6: `Chart.register` and `Chart.unregister` take one List, and `Chart.instances` is a Map from the `ToString` of each id. `Chart.jsClass` is the class as a value with the statics of :105-120 ([Chart.js ADR 0048](adr/0048-entry-points-and-public-library.md) §2). `toBase64Image` returns a `Future<String>` (§8). `resize` and `eventHandler` are fields that hold a function ([Chart.js ADR 0022](adr/0022-chart-test-harness.md) §5). The dataset meta of `getDatasetMeta` is the class `ChartMeta`, also a `types.PropertyAccess` for the animation of `visible`. The `_` members that other modules or the specs read drop the underscore and carry `@internal`. `getDataVisibility` has a port-only early return for an empty `_hiddenIndices`; `toggleDataVisibility` takes any JS value and writes the key `ToString(index)`; `ChartMeta.total` is a typed field, and `Chart.legend` is a field that the legend plugin writes (`plugin.legend.js:602`) ([Chart.js ADR 0028](adr/0028-controller-interfaces.md)). The index reads use `jsGetIndex` ([Chart.js ADR 0036](adr/0036-parsed-data.md)). The six visibility and meta methods `getDatasetMeta`, `isDatasetVisible`, `setDatasetVisibility`, `getDataVisibility`, `hide` and `show` take a `num` index and convert it with `toDouble()` at the entry ([Chart.js ADR 0052](adr/0052-typed-public-api.md) §10). The chart options are a typed view (`OptionView`): typed option reads (readKey, readOption; [Chart.js ADR 0054](adr/0054-typed-internal-model.md) §4). Not a pure layer: it imports the platform layer. The spec has 84/85 cases that pass. `test/port/core/core.controller_test.dart` compares 12 scenarios with upstream and tests the hunks of the port) |
| [`src/core/core.datasetController.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/core/core.datasetController.js) | 1077 | `lib/src/core/core.datasetController.dart` | ported (differences: 4 `adapt`, 1 `platform`, 1 `js-semantics`, [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-typed-contexts), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-typed-parsed), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-typed-element-options), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-component-class-role), [differences.md](differences.md#platform-array-events), [differences.md](differences.md#js-semantics-resolver-objects); `DatasetController` implements `types.DatasetController`. `DatasetController.jsClass` is the class as a value with the statics `defaults`, `datasetElementType` and `dataElementType` ([Chart.js ADR 0020](adr/0020-registry-and-component-classes.md) §3). The constructor takes `new.target` as an optional last parameter (§4). The chart, the meta and the scales are the interfaces of `lib/src/types/index.dart`. `chart.config` is cast to the `Config` of `core.config.dart`. The `_` members that other modules read drop the underscore and carry `@internal`. `_update` is `update_`, and `_getSharedOptions` is `getSharedOptions_` ([Chart.js ADR 0023](adr/0023-underscore-member-collisions.md)). The `_onData*` members are the `onData*` members of `ArrayListener`. The element options are the typed classes of `element_options.dart` ([Chart.js ADR 0021](adr/0021-typed-options-in-the-resolver.md)). `clearStacks` continues after an item without a value, from the commit pin (ledger row #12155). The parse functions give `ParsedItem`; `_stacks` is an `ItemStacks` and the stack objects `StackEntry` (`stacksOf`, `axisStackOf`, `visualValuesOf`, `setStackValue`); the dataset options are an `OptionView`; the contexts are typed `ScriptableContext` objects; `_cachedDataOpts` is two typed Maps ([Chart.js ADR 0054](adr/0054-typed-internal-model.md) §8, §11). A parsed item reads with `parsedValueOf`, and a data item of `parsing: false` and a `_stacks` object that user code gave read with `jsGetKey`; the index reads use `jsGetIndex` and `jsSetIndex`, and the sorted test of `parse` uses `jsLessThan`. `stacksOf` reads the `_stacks` of a List or another object without a property Map ([Chart.js ADR 0036](adr/0036-parsed-data.md)). `resolveDataElementOptions`, `resolveDatasetElementOptions` and `getStyle` return `ElementOptions`, and `_cloneIfNotShared` and `getSharedOptions` copy with `clone`. `updateElement` takes a `types.Element`, and `getLabelAndValue` gives an `Object?` label ([Chart.js ADR 0028](adr/0028-controller-interfaces.md)). `_insertElements` keeps the old parsed item at each new index below the old length, as the upstream `move` does. `_dataCheck` keeps a string `data` as a `JsStringList` with no listener, as upstream (`core.datasetController.js:386`). `ctx` is writable, as the JS property `_ctx` ([Chart.js ADR 0030](adr/0030-controller-test-strategy.md)). `linkScales` keeps the raw axis id of the dataset: the axis ids of `ChartMeta` are `Object?`, and `getScaleForId` reads `chart.scales[ToString(id)]`. `parseObjectData` casts a parsing key at its `resolveObjectKey` read in the loop, as upstream throws there. `test/port/core/core.datasetController_test.dart` compares 34 scenarios with upstream. Not a pure layer: it imports `core.animations`) |
| [`src/core/core.defaults.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/core/core.defaults.js) | 175 | `lib/src/core/core.defaults.dart` | ported (differences: 3 `adapt`, [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-defaults-route), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-defaults-scope-object), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-typed-options); `defaults`, `overrides` and `descriptors` are typed roots, `Defaults` is a `ChartOptionsScope`, and `route` gives a typed scope a typed route ([Chart.js ADR 0054](adr/0054-typed-internal-model.md) §3)) |
| [`src/core/core.element.ts`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/core/core.element.ts) | 45 | `lib/src/core/core.element.dart` | ported (differences: 2 `adapt`, [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-typed-elements), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-component-class-role); [Chart.js ADR 0017](adr/0017-element-properties.md): `Element` with typed properties, the access by name and the Map of extra properties, `ElementProps` and `ElementPoint`. The numeric properties are unboxed `double` fields with a mask of the `undefined` state, `$context` is a typed member, `assign` of a property object of a controller takes the typed setters for an element of a built-in class (the exact-class rule, `typedWrites`), and the ported callers of `getProps` take its typed forms ([Chart.js ADR 0054](adr/0054-typed-internal-model.md) §9). The option conversions of the element classes are in the port-only `lib/src/js/js_webidl.dart` and `jsOrZero` of `lib/src/js/js_number.dart`. `Element.jsClass` is the class as a value with the statics `defaults` and `defaultRoutes` ([Chart.js ADR 0020](adr/0020-registry-and-component-classes.md) §3). The port-only `elementCfgArg` gives the `cfg` of the `construct` closures of the element class values: a Map, or Dart `null` for an absent or falsy `cfg`. Another value throws a `TypeError`. `Element` declares `hidden`, which `DatasetController.draw` reads for each element (Chart.js ADR 0017, Consequences; [performance.md](performance.md) §10.6). It keeps any value, as an extra property does) |

## Plugins (`src/plugins`)

| Upstream file | Lines | Dart target | Status |
|---|---:|---|---|
| [`src/plugins/index.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/plugins/index.js) | 7 | `lib/src/plugins/index.dart` | ported: the seven `export` lines (:1-7) are `export ... show` directives of the plugin objects `colors`, `decimation`, `filler`, `legend`, `subTitle`, `title` and `tooltip`, the export names in lowerCamelCase ([Chart.js ADR 0038](adr/0038-plugin-modules-class-values-and-interfaces.md) §1-§2). The library exports no class `Title`, `Legend` or `Tooltip`. Not a pure layer: `plugin.tooltip.dart` imports `core.animations` |
| [`src/plugins/plugin.colors.ts`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/plugins/plugin.colors.ts) | 127 | `lib/src/plugins/plugin.colors.dart` | ported (differences: 4 `theme`, 1 `js-semantics`, [differences.md](differences.md#theme-H4a), [differences.md](differences.md#theme-H4b), [differences.md](differences.md#theme-H4c), [differences.md](differences.md#theme-H5), [differences.md](differences.md#js-semantics-narrow-datasets); ported in wave 1 of phase 8, registered in wave 4 ([Chart.js ADR 0041](adr/0041-plugin-test-strategy.md) §7). The plugin object `colors` is a typed `Plugin` ([Chart.js ADR 0054](adr/0054-typed-internal-model.md) §12) with the keys `id`, `defaults` and `beforeLayout`. The options are read through the facade `ColorsPluginOptions`. The colorizers write a typed dataset scope through its typed keys and a dataset Map of the user by name. The hunks H4a-H4c and H5 of [Chart.js ADR 0050](adr/0050-public-theme-api.md) give the theme palette: H5 reads it from the theme map of the chart through `chartThemeOf` of `core.config.dart`. The doughnut and polar area colorizers throw a `TypeError` for a `data` that is not a List, as `data.map` does. Not a pure layer: it imports the doughnut and polar area controllers. The colorizers write into the dataset Map of the user: a Map with a narrow value type or an unmodifiable Map throws (row `js-semantics-narrow-datasets`). `test/port/plugins/plugin.colors_test.dart` compares 14 cases with upstream (13 unit cases and the full case `full: radar`)) |
| [`src/plugins/plugin.decimation.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/plugins/plugin.decimation.js) | 287 | `lib/src/plugins/plugin.decimation.dart` | ported (differences: 1 `adapt`, 2 `js-semantics`, [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-decimation-data-accessor), [differences.md](differences.md#js-semantics-narrow-datasets), [differences.md](differences.md#js-semantics-decimation-data); ported in wave 2 of phase 8, registered in wave 4 ([Chart.js ADR 0041](adr/0041-plugin-test-strategy.md) §8). `decimation` is a typed `Plugin` with the upstream keys (`id`, `defaults`, `beforeElementsUpdate`, `destroy`; `destroy` is an extra) ([Chart.js ADR 0054](adr/0054-typed-internal-model.md) §12). The options are read through the facade `DecimationOptions`. The `data` accessor of `plugin.decimation.js:249-265` is a plain entry with the getter value, an `Expando` that marks the dataset and holds the last value that the plugin wrote, and a sync before each use ([Chart.js ADR 0038](adr/0038-plugin-modules-class-values-and-interfaces.md) §8). It reads and writes a typed dataset scope through the keys `_data` and `_decimated` of the dataset scope kinds, and a Map of the user by name. `lttbDecimation` and `minMaxDecimation` are `@internal` for their pure tests. They read the data items with the JS operators: `+=` with `jsAdd`, the arithmetic through `jsToNumber`, `\| 0` with `jsToInt32` and the compare of the raw `y` values with `jsLessThan` ([Chart.js ADR 0036](adr/0036-parsed-data.md)). The `Error` of `:277` is a `StateError`. A pure layer (§9). `test/port/plugins/plugin.decimation_test.dart` and `plugin.decimation_algorithms_test.dart` cover it) |
| [`src/plugins/plugin.filler/filler.drawing.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/plugins/plugin.filler/filler.drawing.js) | 206 | `lib/src/plugins/plugin.filler/filler.drawing.dart` | ported (differences: 1 `adapt`, [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-filler-target-classes); registered in wave 4 of phase 8 ([Chart.js ADR 0041](adr/0041-plugin-test-strategy.md) §8). `drawfill` reads the members of a fill target through `types.LineElement` and `types.SimpleArc`, and another target throws a `TypeError` ([Chart.js ADR 0040](adr/0040-filler-targets-and-plain-points.md) §4). `clipToIndexBounds` (#12283) is in the pin, with `jsMin` and `jsMax`. `first()` gives Dart `null` for the upstream `0`, and the clip reads NaN for it. `clipToIndexBounds` and `interpolatedLineTo` are public with `@visibleForTesting`. The style of a segment reads its `backgroundColor` through the typed `SegmentStyle` of `helpers.segment`, another object by name ([Chart.js ADR 0054](adr/0054-typed-internal-model.md) §9). A pure layer) |

The access by name covers the 28 own names of the class and `_ignoreReplayEvents`: `Element` handles 5 of them, and `operator []` and `operator []=` handle the other 24 (§2). The tooltip reads `TooltipOptions`. The items, body items, label colors, label point styles and positions are typed records (`TooltipItem`, `TooltipBodyItem`, `TooltipLabelStyle`, `TooltipLabelPointStyle`, `TooltipPosition`, [Chart.js ADR 0054](adr/0054-typed-internal-model.md) §12). The positioners are one Map of function values (§4). `Object.assign({}, position, size)` is a record (§5). `update` is a field that holds a function (§6). `setContext` and `override` of a resolver are typed calls, by name for plain options (§7). The noop test of `invokeCallbackWithFallback` gives the result of the two upstream calls (§8). `tooltip` is a typed `Plugin`. Not a pure layer: it imports `core.animations`. `test/port/plugins/plugin.tooltip_test.dart` compares 35 cases with upstream (34 unit cases and the full case)) |

| [`src/types/color.d.ts`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/types/color.d.ts) | 1 | `lib/src/types/color.dart` | distributed |
| [`src/types/geometric.d.ts`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/types/geometric.d.ts) | 52 | `lib/src/types/geometric.dart` | distributed (differences: 1 `adapt`, [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-spline-control-point-nan)) |
| [`src/types/index.d.ts`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/types/index.d.ts) | 3934 | `lib/src/types/index.dart` | distributed (differences: 3 `adapt`, [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-line-points-elements), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-filler-target-classes), [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-types-chart-index-parameters); the commit pin adds the type `ArcSpacingMode`, the key `spacingMode` of the arc and doughnut options, the keys `strokeColor` and `strokeWidth` of the scale title and a scriptable `tickWidth` of the grid (ledger rows #12238, #12130 and #12321). Phase 7 adds the members that the controllers read first, `RadialLinearScale` and a minimal `LegendElement` ([Chart.js ADR 0028](adr/0028-controller-interfaces.md)). Phase 8 adds the members that the plugins read first ([Chart.js ADR 0038](adr/0038-plugin-modules-class-values-and-interfaces.md) §6). Wave 1 adds `Chart.titleBlock` (`plugin.title.js:117`, a field, Dart `null` for `undefined`) and `ChartConfiguration.data` (`core.config.js:177`). Wave 2 makes `Chart.legend` a field and adds `Chart.hide` and `show`, `ChartMeta.visible`, `DatasetController.supportsDecimation`, `Scale.type` and `getBaseValue`, `LineElement.first` (Dart `null` for the upstream `0`), `interpolate` and `pathSegment`, `LegendElement.legendItems`, and the port-only interface `SimpleArc` ([Chart.js ADR 0040](adr/0040-filler-targets-and-plain-points.md) §4). Wave 3 adds `Chart.tooltip` (`index.d.ts:544`, a field, Dart `null` for `undefined`), `Chart.getElementsAtEventForMode` (`index.d.ts:564`, the concrete result stays a `List<InteractionItem>`), `DatasetController.getLabelAndValue` (`index.d.ts:641`, the raw label) and `getDataset` (`index.d.ts:648`), and the interface `TooltipModel` (`index.d.ts:2648`) with the members of [Chart.js ADR 0039](adr/0039-tooltip-model-and-animation.md) §14. `LineElement.updateControlPoints` takes a `ChartArea?`, and `VisualElementRange.getRange` gives an `Object`: the JS `+` of two options can give a string. `spacingMode` is a known key of `ArcOptions` in `element_options.dart`, and ported code reads the other keys from the option Maps. Phase 9 adds the port-only member `LegendElement.legendHitBoxes` (`plugin.legend.js:52`, not in `index.d.ts`): the keyboard of `ChartView` reads the hit boxes through it and imports no plugin ([Chart.js ADR 0051](adr/0051-input-events-and-interaction-state.md) §2). Phase 10 declares the index parameters of `Chart.getDatasetMeta`, `getDataVisibility`, `isDatasetVisible`, `hide` and `show` as `num` ([Chart.js ADR 0052](adr/0052-typed-public-api.md) §10)) |
| [`src/types/layout.d.ts`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/types/layout.d.ts) | 65 | `lib/src/types/layout.dart` | distributed |
| [`src/types/utils.d.ts`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/types/utils.d.ts) | 30 | | n/a: TypeScript type operators (`DeepPartial`, `Merge` and others). Dart has no form for them. |

| [`src/index.ts`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/index.ts) | 25 | `lib/src/index.dart` | ported: `controllers`, `elements`, `plugins`, `scales` and `registerables` (:8-25), and the exports of `controllers/index.js`, `core/index.ts`, `elements/index.js`, `platform/index.js`, `plugins/index.js` and `scales/index.js` (:1-6). The namespaces are unmodifiable Maps with the export names in code unit order, as in the published bundle ([Chart.js ADR 0020](adr/0020-registry-and-component-classes.md) §6). Each namespace has all its entries. `scales` has the six scale classes, not `LinearScaleBase`. `controllers` has the eight controllers of phase 7, and `plugins` has the seven plugins of phase 8 in the order of `src/plugins/index.js:1-7` ([Chart.js ADR 0038](adr/0038-plugin-modules-class-values-and-interfaces.md) §2). Thus `flutter_test_config.dart` registers them with the built-in components. `lib/chartjs.dart` exports this library through `lib/src/types.dart` ([Chart.js ADR 0048](adr/0048-entry-points-and-public-library.md) §4, §5). `test/port/index_test.dart` tests the namespaces |
| [`src/index.umd.ts`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/index.umd.ts) | 54 | `lib/src/index.umd.dart` | ported (differences: 1 `adapt`, 2 `platform`, [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-umd-namespace-objects), [differences.md](differences.md#platform-umd-entry), [differences.md](differences.md#platform-umd-dom-platform)): `chartUmd` of `lib/src/index.umd.dart` (the body of :28-47 at the first read, [Chart.js ADR 0048](adr/0048-entry-points-and-public-library.md) §1). The namespace keys are the upstream names, also the `_` names of `Chart.helpers` and the key `DomPlatform` of `Chart.platforms`. `Chart.jsClass`, `Animation.jsClass`, `Animations.jsClass` and `FlutterPlatform.jsClass` are new class values (§2). No public library exports it. Not a pure layer. `test/port/index.umd_test.dart` compares the keys with the pinned bundle |
| [`src/types.ts`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/src/types.ts) | 55 | `lib/src/types.dart` | ported (differences: 1 `adapt`, [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-types-entry)): `lib/src/types.dart` exports `index.dart` and `types/index.dart` without the 8 interfaces whose names `index.dart` takes ([Chart.js ADR 0048](adr/0048-entry-points-and-public-library.md) §4). The TypeScript-only names of :48-52 have no Dart form. `lib/chartjs.dart` exports it without the names of §6. Not a pure layer |

## Upstream tests

Upstream tests come in two kinds. Both are ported with the module they cover.

- **Specs** (`test/specs`, Jasmine): 54 files, 26,375 lines. They mostly check computed state (scale ticks, element positions, resolved options) and the recorded canvas calls, so they translate to `flutter_test` unit tests. The **Cases** column gives the number of upstream cases of each spec file in [`doc/upstream/manifest.json`](upstream/README.md).
- **Image fixtures** (`test/fixtures`): 1,334 files, of which 667 are PNG reference renders produced by Chrome/Firefox. The port compares no image with these PNGs ([Chart.js ADR 0034](adr/0034-no-display-only-js-exactness.md)). The fixture configs can run as smoke tests. A smoke test passes when one frame renders with no exception. The **Fixtures** column gives the number of fixtures of the fixture groups of each spec file.

| Upstream spec | Lines | Cases | Fixtures | Dart test | Status |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|---|
| [`test/specs/controller.bar.tests.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/test/specs/controller.bar.tests.js) | 1853 | 55 | 87 | `test/specs/controller.bar_test.dart` | ported (55/55 cases pass on the VM. The 3 cases with `plugin.legend` in the chart (`:1426`, `:1442`, `:1538`) also pass with the legend registered ([Chart.js ADR 0030](adr/0030-controller-test-strategy.md) §12 item 6). The 3 cases with a dataset of the type `line` (`:70`, `:310`, `:671`) got their bodies in the lift of wave 2. The case of :1808 reads `chart.tooltip` through `types.TooltipModel` and got its body in wave 4 of phase 8. Each chart case is a `testWidgets` case with the conventions of [Chart.js ADR 0030](adr/0030-controller-test-strategy.md) §13 and the shared matchers and spies of `test/support/jasmine.dart`. The fixture group `auto` calls `fixture.specs` ([Chart.js ADR 0029](adr/0029-fixture-smoke-runner.md)): 70 of its 87 fixtures pass in `test:fixtures`, and 17 skip as `diverges: adapt-date-moment-format` (their ICU twins pass). `test/port/controllers/controller.bar_test.dart` covers the controller) |

### Test set-up

`test/index.js` sets up the upstream specs. Its Dart target is `test/flutter_test_config.dart`
([Chart.js ADR 0012](adr/0012-dart-file-naming.md)). `flutter test` runs it around each test file
of the package.

| Upstream file | Lines | Dart target | Status |
|---|---:|---|---|
| [`test/index.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/Chart.js/blob/v4.6.1/test/index.js) | 36 | `test/flutter_test_config.dart` | ported: DPR 1 (:5), and `releaseCharts` after each test (:34-36) at the unmount of the harness body, with a `tearDown` as the fallback. Before the snapshot of the restore, the file registers the built-in components, as `src/index.umd.ts:28` does. The UTC zone line (:23) is `setDefaultDateZone('Etc/UTC')` of the default date adapter ([Chart.js ADR 0024](adr/0024-dates.md)). `setUpSpecs()` of `pure.dart` makes the same call. After the registration, the colors plugin line (:25-28) sets `defaults.plugins.colors.enabled = false`, before the snapshot of the restore. Upstream runs it once in a top-level `beforeAll`. `jasmine.fixture` (:15-17) is `specs` of `test/fixtures/index.dart`, which the spec files import with the prefix `fixture` ([Chart.js ADR 0029](adr/0029-fixture-smoke-runner.md)). `injectWrapperCSS` and `addMatchers` are n/a; differences: 5 `harness`, [differences.md](differences.md#harness-defaults-restore), [differences.md](differences.md#harness-foreign-audit), [differences.md](differences.md#harness-test-font), [differences.md](differences.md#harness-release-at-unmount), [differences.md](differences.md#harness-register-builtins) |

## chartjs-test-utils 0.4.0

The test utilities of Chart.js, [chartjs-test-utils](https://github.com/chartjs/chartjs-test-utils/tree/v0.4.0)
(MIT, chartjs-test-utils Contributors), the version of the `package.json` of the pinned Chart.js. The
pinned `third_party/chartjs-test-utils` submodule holds the source. The port is the harness package
`packages/chartjs_test_utils` ([Chart.js ADR 0035](adr/0035-third-party-sources.md),
[porting-flow.md](porting-flow.md) §3). `tool/port_sources.yaml` maps each file. Each ported file
starts with `// Ported from chartjs-test-utils v0.4.0 <path> (MIT, chartjs-test-utils Contributors).`
The Dart targets are relative to `packages/chartjs_test_utils`.

| Upstream file | Lines | Dart target | Status |
|---|---:|---|---|
| [`src/context.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/chartjs-test-utils/blob/v0.4.0/src/context.js) | 168 | `lib/src/context.dart` | ported (differences: 2 `harness`, [differences.md](differences.md#harness-mock-optional-args), [differences.md](differences.md#harness-index-pure-split)). The port-only `roundRect` records its calls as the other path methods do, because `addRoundedRectPath` calls it ([differences.md](differences.md#adapt-rounded-rect-path)). The file also holds `createMockContext` of `src/index.js:33`, so that `pure.dart` stays a pure layer (Chart.js ADR 0022, section 1) |
| [`src/fixture.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/chartjs-test-utils/blob/v0.4.0/src/fixture.js) | 93 | `lib/src/fixture.dart` | ported: `specsFromFixtures` and `specFromFixture` as a smoke runner ([Chart.js ADR 0029](adr/0029-fixture-smoke-runner.md)): the prefix match, the plugin rule, the `run` hook, one frame of `pumpFrame`, no image comparison. The list and the pure data configs come from tool 4, the impure configs from the hand ports of `flutter_chartjs_port` (`test/fixtures/hand/`, the registry `test/fixtures/index.dart`). `FixtureSource` gives the files. Before a config loads, the runner compares the SHA-256 of the upstream file in `third_party/Chart.js/test/fixtures/` with the manifest. Without the checkout, each fixture skips; with `FIXTURES_REQUIRED=1`, it fails. Each fixture test has the tag `fixtures`. Not a pure layer. `test/fixture_test.dart` tests it through a probe run (differences: 2 `harness`, [differences.md](differences.md#harness-fixture-sources), [differences.md](differences.md#harness-fixture-frame)) |
| [`src/index.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/chartjs-test-utils/blob/v0.4.0/src/index.js) | 61 | `lib/src/index.dart` | partial: `acquireChart`, `releaseChart`, `releaseCharts` and the registry `charts` (:9-31, :54-61), and the re-exports `afterEvent`, `waitForResize` and `triggerMouseEvent` of `src/utils.js` (:6). `package:chartjs_test_utils/flutter.dart` exports them. `index.dart` imports `utils.dart` with the prefix `utils`, because the Dart names of `_acquireChart` and `_releaseChart` are the names of this file ([Chart.js ADR 0012](adr/0012-dart-file-naming.md)). `releaseCharts` never deletes a chart, as upstream. `index.dart` is not a pure layer, because it imports `utils.dart` and `core.controller.dart`. `createMockContext` is in `lib/src/context.dart` ([differences.md](differences.md#harness-index-pure-split)). `index.dart` keeps a comment at the place of `:33`. The re-export of `src/fixture.js` (:5) is `export 'fixture.dart';`. `injectWrapperCSS` is n/a: the DOM stand-in `lib/src/dom.dart` gives the class `chartjs-wrapper` the effect of its rule. `addMatchers` is n/a: the matchers are imported functions. `test/index_test.dart` tests the registry |
| [`src/matchers.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/chartjs-test-utils/blob/v0.4.0/src/matchers.js) | 230 | `lib/src/matchers.dart` | ported: `toBeCloseToPixel`, `toBeCloseToPoint`, `toEqualOneOf`, `toBeValidChart`, `toBeChartOfSize` (package:matcher Matchers; differences: 1 `harness`, [differences.md](differences.md#harness-chart-matchers)). The two chart matchers read only pure interfaces, with the display size rule of [Chart.js ADR 0022](adr/0022-chart-test-harness.md) §9. `toEqualImageData` and its image helpers are n/a ([Chart.js ADR 0034](adr/0034-no-display-only-js-exactness.md)) |
| [`src/matchers.options.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/chartjs-test-utils/blob/v0.4.0/src/matchers.options.js) | 113 | `lib/src/matchers.options.dart` | ported (differences: 1 `harness`, [differences.md](differences.md#harness-expected-element)) |
| [`src/spriting.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/chartjs-test-utils/blob/v0.4.0/src/spriting.js) | 101 | | n/a: no image comparison ([Chart.js ADR 0034](adr/0034-no-display-only-js-exactness.md)) |
| [`src/utils.js`](https://github.com/chartjs/chartjs-test-utils/blob/v0.4.0/src/utils.js) | 184 | `lib/src/utils.dart` | ported: `_acquireChart` and `_releaseChart` as `acquireChart` and `releaseChart` (:36-115, [Chart.js ADR 0022](adr/0022-chart-test-harness.md) §4), `waitForResize` and `afterEvent` (:130-149, line-by-line ports over the function fields of `Chart`, §5), and `_resolveElementPoint` and `triggerMouseEvent` (:151-184, §6). `acquireChart` works only in the body of a `testWidgets` test. A forced layout of the harness body attaches the chart at its construction. `triggerMouseEvent` dispatches a `NativeEvent` to the listeners of the canvas and pumps frames of 16 ms. `createCanvas`, `readImageData` and `injectCSS` are n/a. Not a pure layer: it imports `core.controller` and the chart host. `test/utils_test.dart` tests the helpers (differences: 2 `harness`, [differences.md](differences.md#harness-acquire-chart), [differences.md](differences.md#harness-trigger-mouse-event)) |

## Port-only files

Files with no Chart.js counterpart. Each file starts with
`// Original code, not ported from Chart.js: <reason>.`, except the fdlibm ports, which have
the rule-2 headers of their own source. The fdlibm reference copies are in `third_party/fdlibm/`.
The reference engine of `lib/src/js` is V8 13.6 in Node.js v24.22.1 on arm64. The reference bits
of `jsLog10`, `jsAtan2`, `jsSin` and `jsCos` are the bits of the fdlibm data of V8 13.6 in
Node.js v24.12.0 on x64 ([Chart.js ADR 0045](adr/0045-reference-engine-arm64.md) §2).

| File | Contents | Tests |
|---|---|---|
| `lib/src/js/js_math.dart` | `Math` and `Number` builtins with the results of the reference engine: `numberEpsilon`, `jsRound`, `jsToInt32`, `jsMax`, `jsMin`, `jsSquare`, `jsLog10`, `jsAtan2`, `jsSin`, `jsCos`. `jsSquare(x)` is `Math.pow(x, 2)`: `x * x` (Chart.js ADR 0045). `jsMax` and `jsMin` are `Math.max` and `Math.min` for `double` operands (Chart.js ADR 0045). On dart2wasm they compare the operands in their own code (`jsMaxMinCompare`), because the generic `dart:math` `max` and `min` box `double` operands there. On the VM and dart2js they call `dart:math`. `jsSin` and `jsCos` are `Math.sin` and `Math.cos` at the protected sites ([Chart.js ADR 0045](adr/0045-reference-engine-arm64.md)). Ported code calls `dart:math` `pow` (Chart.js ADR 0045), and `dart:math` `sin` and `cos` with `js-ok` at the display-only sites (Chart.js ADR 0045) | `test/port/js/js_math_test.dart`: `jsMax` and `jsMin` against `dart:math` `max` and `min`, bit for bit, over a grid of 40 operands in both orders (signed zeros, NaN, infinities, subnormals, random finites), and which code each test leg runs. It also pins `dart:math` `max` and `min` against the JS results, and `jsSquare` against `dart:math` `pow(x, 2.0)`, bit for bit, over the grid, overflow values near 1.4e154 and 20,000 seeded random bit patterns |
| `lib/src/js/js_number.dart` | The JS coercions: `jsToNumber`, `jsParseFloat`, `jsIsNaN`, `jsIsFinite`, `jsTruthy` (ToBoolean), `jsToString` (ToString), `jsNumberToString` (Number::toString, radix 10), `jsAdd` (the operator `a + b`: when one operand gives a string, the `ToString` of both operands join, else the numbers add) `jsOrZero` (the form `value \|\| 0` before arithmetic, [Chart.js ADR 0017](adr/0017-element-properties.md) section 8) and `jsLessThan` (the operator `a < b` on raw values: two strings compare by their code units, a `DateTime` by its time value, [Chart.js ADR 0036](adr/0036-parsed-data.md)). Two doubles take a fast path in `jsAdd`. A `DateTime` is a JS `Date`: `jsToNumber` gives its time value ([Chart.js ADR 0024](adr/0024-dates.md)), and `jsToString` gives the `Date.prototype.toString` text in the platform zone, with the V8 zone name on the web and in UTC on the VM (Chart.js ADR 0036; the row [`platform-date-zone-name`](differences.md#platform-date-zone-name)) | `test/port/js/js_number_test.dart`: `jsAdd` against `a + b` of Node.js v24.12.0 on x64, the `ToNumber` and the `ToString` of a `DateTime`, 29 comparisons of `jsLessThan`, also 10,000 inputs of `jsNumberToString`, string for string against `String(x)` of the reference engine, and `jsToNumber` of each string of 0-5 characters from `0-9 . + - e E` (0-3 characters and a sample on the web) against a `StrDecimalLiteral` oracle, bit for bit; `test/port/js/js_number_chart_test.dart`: the layout of `Date` labels of a category scale in UTC, and the NaN arc of a polar area `angle` that resolves to a plain object |
| `lib/src/js/js_undefined.dart` | The JS value `undefined` as a value: `Undefined` and its one instance `undefined` (Chart.js ADR 0043, "The sentinel") | `test/port/js/js_number_test.dart`, `test/port/js/js_array_test.dart` |
| `lib/src/js/js_function.dart` | The JS call of a function value: `JsFunction` (a closure that gets `this` as its first parameter), `jsIsFunction` (`typeof value === 'function'`, also true for a class value of `js_class.dart`, [Chart.js ADR 0020](adr/0020-registry-and-component-classes.md) §2), `jsCall` (drops the extra arguments, pads the missing required arguments with `undefined`, passes `this` to a `JsFunction` only, and throws an `ArgumentError` for a class value, which needs `new`), `jsCall1` (`jsCall` with one argument, without the argument list on the VM) and `jsMaxArity`. On the VM, `jsCall` calls a closure that takes 0 to 4 arguments with a dynamic call, because `Function.apply` is about 10 times slower there. It uses `Function.apply` for 5 to 8 arguments and on the web. Its library documentation states the convention for users | `test/port/js/js_function_test.dart`: each arity from 0 to 8 with 0 to 10 arguments, with and without `this`, on both sides of the dynamic-call limit. `jsCall1` gives the `jsCall` results and errors for each arity, `this`, NaN, -0, `null` and `undefined`. A class value is a function for `jsIsFunction`, and `jsCall` and `jsCall1` throw for it |
| `lib/src/js/js_object.dart` | The JS property read `object[key]`: `jsGet`. It gives the `get` trap of a resolver, the `jsRead` of a `JsObjectFace` (the face by name of a typed object of [Chart.js ADR 0054](adr/0054-typed-internal-model.md) §5: `jsRead`, `jsWrite`, `jsKeys`, `jsHas`, `jsDelete`), the value of a Map key or `undefined` for an absent key, the canonical indices and the `length` of a List or a String, the static property of a class value ([Chart.js ADR 0020](adr/0020-registry-and-component-classes.md) §2), and `undefined` for other values. It throws a `TypeError` for `null` and `undefined`. The ported modules call it in place of their private copies. `jsGet` carries the marker of the `differences.md` row [`js-semantics-resolver-objects`](differences.md#js-semantics-resolver-objects): it knows only Map, List and String values. The frozen state of `Object.freeze` ([Chart.js ADR 0021](adr/0021-typed-options-in-the-resolver.md) §5): the interface `JsFreezable` (`isFrozen`, `freeze`), `jsObjectFreeze` (`Object.freeze`: freezes a `JsFreezable`, returns its argument) and `jsObjectIsFrozen` (`Object.isFrozen`: the state of a `JsFreezable`, true for a primitive, false for each other value). Both carry the marker of the `differences.md` row [`js-semantics-object-freeze`](differences.md#js-semantics-object-freeze): only a `JsFreezable` freezes. `jsTypeof` is `typeof value` ([Chart.js ADR 0019](adr/0019-animation.md) §1): `'function'` for each value of `jsIsFunction`, also a class value, and `'object'` for JS `null`. `jsGetKey` is the read `object[key]` of a parsed item, a data item or a stack object, with the `jsGet` result and a Map case first. `jsGetIndex` is the read `object[index]` with a number index: a List gives the item at a whole index in range, else `undefined` ([Chart.js ADR 0036](adr/0036-parsed-data.md)) | `test/port/js/js_object_test.dart`, with expected values from Node.js v24.12.0 on x64: the reads, also of a class value, `jsObjectFreeze` (a `JsFreezable`, a primitive, the pin of `js-semantics-object-freeze` with a plain Map) and `jsObjectIsFrozen` (primitives, a `JsFreezable`, a plain Map, a `Map.unmodifiable`, a List, a function and a class value), `jsTypeof` for each kind of value, and `jsGetKey` and `jsGetIndex` against `jsGet`, also on a resolver and a context proxy |
| `lib/src/js/js_webidl.dart` | The WebIDL conversions that the DOM applies to the value of a Canvas 2D setter ([Chart.js ADR 0017](adr/0017-element-properties.md) section 8): `canvasStyle` (`fillStyle`, `strokeStyle`: the value as it is), `canvasNumber` (an `unrestricted double` such as `lineWidth`: `ToNumber`), `canvasKeyword` (a `DOMString` keyword such as `lineCap`: `ToString`, JS `null` gives Dart `null`) and `canvasLineDash` (the `sequence<unrestricted double>` of `setLineDash`: `ToNumber` of each item, a `TypeError` for a value that is not a List). The `undefined` of an absent option gives Dart `null`, which the canvas layer ignores, as the DOM ignores the value. The element classes call them. The dataset controllers and the plugins call them too | `test/port/js/js_webidl_test.dart` |
| `test/port/helpers/helpers.core_test.dart` | `helpers.core` behavior that `test/specs/helpers.core.tests.js` does not check (AGENTS.md rule 3), with expected values from `src/helpers/helpers.core.ts` in Node.js v24.12.0 on x64. One test pins a port difference of Chart.js ADR 0043: a class instance is not an object. The groups `adapt-merge-element-target`, `js-semantics-resolver-cache`, `js-semantics-to-upper-case` and `js-semantics-set-equality` pin the `differences.md` rows of the same names | — |
| `test/port/helpers/helpers.config_test.dart` | `helpers.config` behavior that `test/specs/helpers.config.tests.js` does not check (AGENTS.md rule 3). The group `null versus undefined` holds the resolver tests of Chart.js ADR 0043: for the plain, prefixed, `_fallback`, scriptable, indexable, `_cached`, sub-resolver, key enumeration, set and descriptor paths, an absent key and the `undefined` sentinel resolve alike, and `null` stops the resolution. One group shows that a stored sentinel blocks `mergeIf`. Other groups cover array scopes, `Object.keys` of a resolver through `jsObjectKeys` (also in `each`, `clone` and `merge`), `resolveObjectKey` of a prefixed resolver, a delete on a frozen scope, the `this` of the descriptor functions and `_parseObjectDataRadialScale`. The groups `adapt-resolver-cached-read` and `js-semantics-resolver-objects` pin the `differences.md` rows of the same names. The expected values come from `src/helpers/helpers.config.ts` and `src/helpers/helpers.core.ts` in Node.js v24.12.0 on x64 | — |
| `lib/src/js/js_array.dart` | `Array.prototype.sort` and `Object.keys`: `jsSort` (stable `mergeSort`, comparator sign, Chart.js ADR 0045) and `jsObjectKeys` (array-index keys first). `jsArrayIndex` is the one canonical array-index parser. `jsObjectKeys`, `jsGet` and the `in` test of `helpers.config` use it. `JsProxyObject` is a JS `Proxy` as a `Map`: the resolvers of `helpers.config` implement it, and `jsObjectKeys` and `jsGet` call its traps (Chart.js ADR 0043). `jsOwnPropertyNames` is `Object.getOwnPropertyNames`: the `ownKeys` trap of a resolver, else `jsObjectKeys` ([Chart.js ADR 0019](adr/0019-animation.md) §1). `jsSplice` is `Array.prototype.splice`, with the `ToIntegerOrInfinity` of the start and the count. `jsSetIndex` is the write `list[index] = value` of `parse`: a write at the length appends, a write past the length or at a negative index throws a `RangeError` (the row [`platform-array-events`](differences.md#platform-array-events), [Chart.js ADR 0036](adr/0036-parsed-data.md)). `JsStringList` is a string as an array-like value: the reads `s[i]` (one UTF-16 code unit) and `s.length`, and a `TypeError` for each write. `_dataCheck` keeps a string `data` in it ([Chart.js ADR 0036](adr/0036-parsed-data.md) §5) | `test/port/js/js_array_test.dart`, with expected values from Node.js v24.12.0 on x64, also `jsOwnPropertyNames` of a Map and of a resolver, `jsSplice`, `jsSetIndex` and `JsStringList` |
| `test/port/js/number_to_string_reference.dart` | Generated data of `js_number_test.dart` | written by `tool/reference/number_to_string_reference.mjs` |
| `tool/reference/number_to_string_reference.mjs` | Dev-time Node.js generator of `number_to_string_reference.dart`. It runs on the reference engine of Chart.js ADR 0045 §2 | — |

| `packages/chartjs_test_utils/lib/pure.dart` | The pure part of the harness. `setUpSpecs()` is the spec set-up for `dart test`. It calls `setDefaultDateZone('Etc/UTC')` (the port of `test/index.js:23`, [Chart.js ADR 0024](adr/0024-dates.md)), `removeBuiltins()` and `setUpDefaultsRestore()`. `setUpBuiltins(register, unregister)` takes a snapshot of the scopes and registers the built-in components: `test/flutter_test_config.dart` calls it before `main()` (`harness-register-builtins`). `removeBuiltins()` unregisters them and puts back that snapshot. Thus a pure-layer file has the same state under `flutter test` and `dart test`. A `setUpAll` takes a snapshot of `defaults`, `overrides` and `descriptors`, and a `tearDown` restores it after each test. The snapshot keeps the identity of each object and array, the `undefined` sentinel, the routes of each `RoutedScope` and the `RoutedScope` that took the place of each plain Map. `test/flutter_test_config.dart` calls `setUpDefaultsRestore()` too. Thus under `flutter test` a pure spec restores twice, and the second restore changes nothing. It also exports `createMockContext` (of `lib/src/context.dart`, `harness-index-pure-split`), `MockContext`, `ContextCall`, `toBeChartOfSize`, `toBeCloseToPixel`, `toBeCloseToPoint`, `toBeValidChart`, `toEqualOneOf` and `toEqualOptions` | `test/port/core/core.defaults_test.dart`, group `harness restore`; `test/port/flutter_test_config_test.dart`, groups `harness-defaults-restore` and `harness-register-builtins`; `packages/chartjs_test_utils/test/pure_test.dart` |

| `lib/src/canvas/canvas_ui.dart` | The `dart:ui` canvas layer (Chart.js ADR 0013 and 0015): `UiCanvasElement` (an `HTMLCanvasElement` and `CanvasNode`: width and height in device pixels, which reset the context when set; a map-backed `UiCssStyleDeclaration`; `getContext2d()`/`getContext('2d')`; `parentNode` for the host; `toImageSource()`, a snapshot of the bitmap for `createPattern`/`drawImage`, where the DOM takes the canvas element; `toDataURL`, a Future of the PNG data URL of the snapshot at the call, `'data:,'` for a canvas with no pixels ([differences.md](differences.md#platform-to-base64-image), [Chart.js ADR 0048](adr/0048-entry-points-and-public-library.md) §8)) and `UiCanvasContext` (the `CanvasRenderingContext2D` over `ui.PictureRecorder`). The element has the `width` and `height` attributes (`getAttribute`, `setAttribute`, `removeAttribute`). A write of `width` or `height` sets the attribute. A removal gives 300 or 150. `setAttribute` parses a non-negative integer. A value that does not parse gives the default. The `style` attribute reflects the inline style ([Chart.js ADR 0022](adr/0022-chart-test-harness.md) section 4): `setAttribute('style', text)` replaces the declarations, and `getAttribute('style')` gives the text as it was set, or the serialization after a CSSOM write. `UiCssStyleDeclaration` has `cssText` and `styleAttribute`. Another attribute keeps its value as a string. Retained-bitmap model: the content is the pictures since the last full clear, recorded in bitmap (device) pixels. `paint` draws them with the scale that the host gives for each axis. A DPR change keeps the bitmap, as in the DOM. A full `clearRect` (axis-aligned, no clip) drops them. A partial clear or a composite operation other than `source-over` makes `paint` draw them inside a layer. More than 8 pictures flatten with `toImageSync`. `onChanged` fires once until the next `paint`. State save/restore uses pooled states. The clip is part of the state and is replayed onto each new recording. The recording canvas gets a save only for a state with a clip of its own, at its first clip, and a new recording replays a save only for such a state ([canvas-cuts-plan.md](canvas-cuts-plan.md) §7, N1). A gradient or pattern text never takes the translation-only fast path, so its shader stays in user space. `setLineDash` keeps the current list when the value does not change. A dash of length 0 with round or square caps draws the caps as dots. The circle batch (Chart.js ADR 0013, section 8.1): `fill()` and the undashed `stroke()` of a pending full circle with a plain color and `source-over` join a `CircleBatch` (radius plus half the line width at most 48 bitmap pixels, center within 2^23 bitmap pixels; the color times `globalAlpha`; bilinear atlas sampling). A center of NaN draws nothing. Each other access to the recording canvas, a clip, the `restore` of a state with a clip of its own and the end of a recording draw the run first; a `save` and the `restore` of a state with no clip of its own do not. A full clear drops it. The rounded rectangle fast paths ([porting-flow.md](porting-flow.md) §1.1, [differences.md](differences.md#adapt-rounded-rect-path)): `fill` of one `roundRect` draws `drawRRect`, and `clip` of one `roundRect` is a `clipRRect` clip (a third clip kind next to the rectangle and the path clip, also replayed onto each new recording). `fill('evenodd')` of two `rect` or `roundRect` shapes draws `drawDRRect` when one shape is inside the other (`rrectContains`) and the inner shape has a width and a height. When one of two finite `rect` or `roundRect` shapes has no width or no height, `fill` draws the other shape alone with `drawRect` (radii 0) or `drawRRect`, for both fill rules (`_soleShape`): the border ring of a bar that is narrower or lower than its two borders ([performance.md](performance.md) §13.6). A fill of one rectangle, one rounded rectangle or a sole shape with no area makes no draw call ([canvas-cuts-plan.md](canvas-cuts-plan.md) §7, N2). `stroke` keeps the path. `debugPath`, `debugRing`, `debugSoleShape` and `debugClips` show the selection to the tests | `test/port/canvas/canvas_ui_test.dart`; `test/port/canvas/canvas_ui_round_rect_test.dart`; `test/port/canvas/canvas_ui_native_test.dart` |
| `lib/src/canvas/canvas_ui_path.dart` | `UiTransform` (the CTM with the flags axis-aligned, similarity and invertible) and `UiPathBuilder` (a `CanvasPath` that builds a `ui.Path` in bitmap space; the transform applies when a point is added). Non-finite arguments and a transform that is not invertible do nothing. `lineTo`/curves on an empty path start a subpath at their first point. `arc` uses `ui.Path.arcTo` when the transform keeps the arc circular or axis-aligned (any sweep over pi is two half arcs, so a sweep just under 2pi draws a near-full circle), else cubic Béziers. One full circle on an empty path under a similarity transform stays pending (`singleCircle`), so that the context fills and strokes it with `drawCircle`; a later command or a read of the path adds it as arcs. A negative radius throws a `RangeError`. `arcTo` is the tangent arc. `canvasArcSweep` canonicalizes the angles as Chromium does. `uiPathOf` caches the user-space `ui.Path` of a `Path2D`. `roundRect` follows `roundRectGeometry`: under an axis-aligned transform, it adds one `ui.Path.addRRect` when the path goes clockwise in bitmap space, else the HTML segments (a line and an arc for each corner); then a new subpath starts at (x, y). `singleRRect` (one `roundRect`) and `shapePair` (two `rect` or `roundRect` commands) select the fast paths of the context. `rrectContains` is a sufficient test that one rounded rectangle is inside another: the bounds with no tolerance, the corner arcs within 1e-9 of a radius | `test/port/canvas/canvas_ui_test.dart`; `test/port/canvas/canvas_ui_round_rect_test.dart` |
| `lib/src/canvas/canvas_ui_paint.dart` | The styles: `parseCssColor` (the ported @kurkle/color, with a cache of 512 entries; invalid colors are `null`), `serializeCssColor` (`#rrggbb`, or `rgba(r, g, b, a)`), `compositeOperations` (DOM name to `ui.BlendMode`), `UiImageSource`, `UiCanvasGradient` (linear, radial two-point conical and conic; `addColorStop` throws a `RangeError` or a `FormatException`; the shader is built at draw time with the transform of the draw and cached per matrix), `UiCanvasPattern` (`ui.ImageShader` with repeated or decal tile modes) and `dashPath` (`PathMetric.extractPath`; the pattern restarts at each subpath; the initial dash phase follows Skia's `find_first_interval`, so `[0, n]` starts with a dot; with `caps`, a dash of length 0 adds a short segment along the tangent, so that round or square caps draw as dots) | `test/port/canvas/canvas_ui_test.dart` |
| `lib/src/canvas/canvas_ui_text.dart` | Canvas text. `parseCanvasFont` parses the CSS shorthand that `toFontString` gives into a `CanvasFont`. It reads at most 4 style, variant, weight and stretch keywords, each at most one time, as Blink `ConsumeFont` reads them. A weight is a CSS number (`+500`, `5e2`) in [1, 1000] and gives the nearest `ui.FontWeight`. Thus the test font gives the faces of Chrome on Linux with system fonts (regular up to 549.99, bold from 550). `oblique` takes an angle (`deg`, `grad`, `rad`, `turn`) with a number in [-90, 90], and `oblique 0deg` is normal. Then the parse reads a size in px or pt, an optional line height, and a quoted or unquoted family list that ends at the first generic family. Generic families stay family names. A cache keeps each parse. An unparsable font gives `null`. `layoutCanvasText` has an LRU cache of 512 `ui.Paragraph`s keyed by font, text, direction and paint. It disposes an evicted paragraph. The context disposes the uncached paragraph of a gradient or pattern text after the draw. A recorded `ui.Picture` keeps its own copy of the painted text, so the recording still paints both. `buildForeground` builds the stroke `ui.Paint` on a cache miss only. `measureCanvasText` has an LRU cache of 2048 metrics entries. `prepareCanvasText` changes ASCII whitespace to spaces. `canvasBaselineShift` puts top, middle and bottom on the em box scaled from the font ascent and descent. It puts hanging at 80% of the ascent, and it takes ideographic from the paragraph The metrics cache of `measureCanvasText` keeps 32768 entries, and the two LRU caches remove their oldest quarter in one pass when they are full: a category axis measures the cycle of all its labels again on each update ([performance.md](performance.md) §11.7) | `test/port/canvas/canvas_ui_test.dart`; `test/port/canvas/canvas_ui_text_test.dart` |
| `lib/src/canvas/canvas_ui_batch.dart` | `CircleBatch` (Chart.js ADR 0013, section 8.1; [performance.md](performance.md) section 5.4): a run of consecutive full-circle fills and strokes with one radius, one fill color, and one stroke color and width, in bitmap space, drawn with one `drawRawAtlas` call in the order of the calls. The atlas holds the filled and the stroked circle at 4 x 4 sub-pixel offsets, so each sprite draws at a whole pixel (a center moves at most 1/8 pixel, a display-only difference of Chart.js ADR 0034). The atlas samples bilinearly: no resampling at a whole device pixel, and the centers stay in place at a fractional device pixel or under a scale. A run of fewer than 16 circles draws with `drawCircle`. `maxBatchRadius` (the radius plus half the line width) is 48 bitmap pixels. `maxBatchCenter` is 2^23 bitmap pixels (float32 atlas transforms). An LRU cache of 32 atlas images disposes the image that it evicts | `test/port/canvas/canvas_ui_test.dart` |
| `lib/src/canvas/canvas.dart` | The Canvas 2D interface (Chart.js ADR 0013), a pure layer: `CanvasPath`, `CanvasRenderingContext2D` (the 24 methods and 9 setters of the mock context of chartjs-test-utils `src/context.js`, plus the members that upstream source calls), `HTMLCanvasElement` (with `getAttribute`, `setAttribute` and `removeAttribute` for `initCanvas` and `releaseContext`, and `toDataURL`, a `Future<String>`, for `toBase64Image`), `CSSStyleDeclaration`, `TextMetrics`, `CanvasImageSource`, `CanvasFillRule`, the port-only `CanvasNode` and `CanvasContainer` (the parent node of a canvas and its content box, `platform-dom-container`), and `Path2D` (records `PathCommand`s, `replay`, the `cache` slot, `version`). `CanvasPath.roundRect` (lib.dom.d.ts; a number or a list of 1 to 4 numbers, no `DOMPointInit`) is the port-only call of `addRoundedRectPath` ([differences.md](differences.md#adapt-rounded-rect-path)). `roundRectGeometry` gives its rectangle and radii: the HTML steps 1 to 11, with the flips of Chromium and Firefox for a negative width or height. `Path2D.roundRect` examines the arguments at the call and records a `RoundRectCommand`. It re-exports `CanvasGradient` and `CanvasPattern` of `lib/src/types/color.dart` | `test/port/canvas/canvas_test.dart`; `packages/chartjs_test_utils/test/context_test.dart` |
| `lib/src/platform/native_event.dart` | `NativeEvent`, the stand-in for the DOM `Event` (Chart.js ADR 0037): `type`, `offsetX`/`offsetY` in logical chart pixels (NaN for touch events, which have no offset), `touches` (`NativeTouch` records sorted by pointer id), `target` (the canvas) and the opaque `source` (the Flutter `PointerEvent`, or the `KeyEvent` of the keyboard of `ChartView`: the types `keydown`, `keypress` and `keyup` with NaN offsets, [Chart.js ADR 0051](adr/0051-input-events-and-interaction-state.md) §5). | `lib/src/platform/platform.flutter.dart` | Replaces `platform.dom.js` (Chart.js ADR 0037) with the upstream names and `///` lines that name the `platform.dom.js` lines. `initCanvas` and `releaseContext` line by line over the canvas attributes (`getAttribute`, `setAttribute`, `removeAttribute`), the `$chartjs` expando, one throttled proxy per event type with the `chart.ctx != null` guard, `CanvasMutationObserver` attach/detach observers, one `throttled<Size?>` resize function per chart (null = DPR change), `getDevicePixelRatio` from the host (`MediaQuery`), `getMaximumSize` with the unbounded-constraints rule, `isAttached` from the host | `test/port/platform/platform.flutter_test.dart` |
| `lib/src/platform/chart_host.dart` | The internal chart host ([Chart.js ADR 0037](adr/0037-legend-and-tooltip-input-path.md)): `ChartHost` (`LeafRenderObjectWidget`) and `RenderChartHost` (`RenderBox`, repaint boundary, `CanvasContainer`, `MouseTrackerAnnotation`). Synchronous first size at the first layout, later size and DPR changes through `ContainerResizeObserver`, the first notification of an observer at the next layout of the host ([Chart.js ADR 0022](adr/0022-chart-test-harness.md) section 10; a `disconnect` before it gives none, and a new host takes over the waiting observers), net attach/detach through one post-frame callback, `flushCanvasMutations` (the mutation checkpoint of the harness: it reports at once the net attached state of each host with a pending connection check), `TickerMode` via `onTickerModeChanged`, dry layout and intrinsics from the canvas display size (the two px styles, else the render size), clip of a larger chart, paint maps the bitmap to the display size (one scale for each axis, display size / bitmap size, exactly 1 / DPR within 1e-3). The canvas `EventTarget` (`addCanvasEventListener`, `dispatchCanvasEvent`). Pointer events map to the DOM names of Chrome by `PointerDeviceKind` (Chart.js ADR 0037 sections 6 to 10), with no claim in the gesture arena: parent scrollables keep their drags, and tappable ancestors also get the tap. The mouse-like kinds are mouse and trackpad. All other kinds in contact (touch, stylus, inverted stylus, unknown) are touch-like, as in Chrome on Android (the device check of Chart.js ADR 0037, Evidence E8, D5). A touch-like pointer gives the boundary events `pointerover`, `pointerenter`, `pointerout` and `pointerleave`. The Chromium rules of a touch sequence (sections 6 and 8): a move with no change of position, pressure, radii, orientation or tilt gives no event (D2); the tap candidate gives only `pointermove` inside its slop limit (D1); the slop limit is the touch slop of `MediaQuery.gestureSettings`, times 1.5 for a stylus, plus 0.05; the first move past a slop limit, or any move with a change after more than 2 touch-like pointers, begins the pan: `pointermove`, `touchmove`, then `pointercancel`, `pointerout` and `pointerleave` at the view origin for each touch-like pointer of the host, then only `touch*` events until all of them lift (D3, also after a long press). The click rule of a mouse: a down with the primary button and the up of that pointer inside the box give `click` at any distance. The tap rule (one touch-like pointer on the host, no pan, no cancel, the up before `kLongPressTimeout`) gives the compatibility mouse events `mouseover` and `mouseenter` (when the compatibility mouse is not over the host), `mousemove`, `mousedown`, `mouseup` and `click` at the tap point: the down position (D4), the up position for a stylus and an inverted stylus (the device re-check, row STN10: Chrome gives a pen and an eraser no touch adjustment); a tap point outside the box gives the host no compatibility event and moves the compatibility mouse off its owner. A long press gives `mouseover`, `mouseenter` and `mousemove` at the down position before the lift, and no `click`. One compatibility mouse for each isolate: a tap or a long press outside gives `mouseout` and `mouseleave` at its tap point or its down position to the host of the last tap through a static global pointer route, which exists only while an owner exists. A touch-like down on a host also ends the tap of a finger outside. The detach or the dispose of the owner ends it without an event. A mouse up or cancel outside the box gives no event. The host tracks the hover of each kind but the mouse itself for the enter and exit events, for each device, because the engine gives each hover event the pointer id 0 (the hover of a mouse does not end the hover of a stylus), and ignores the `MouseTracker` enter and exit of these kinds. A new host that takes over the canvas in the same frame takes over the attached state and the resize observers of the old host. `onFirstLayout` ([Chart.js ADR 0049](adr/0049-chart-view-widget.md) §2): the owner of the canvas starts its chart in the first layout of the host, after the attach report and before the host takes its size, again after a canvas swap | `test/port/platform/chart_host_test.dart`, `test/port/platform/index_test.dart` |
| `packages/chartjs_test_utils/lib/flutter.dart` | The harness part that needs the Flutter engine (flutter test only): exports `installTestFont`, `testFontFamilies`, `testFontFiles`, and the DOM stand-in of `lib/src/dom.dart`: `document`, `window`, `HarnessDocument`, `HarnessElement`, `HarnessWindow`, `setTimeout`, `afterEachChart`, `pumpFrames`, `pumpFrame`, `waitForDone`, `frameInterval`, `specTimeout` and the extensions `CanvasParentElement` and `CssProperties`. It also exports the chart helpers of `lib/src/index.dart`: `acquireChart`, `releaseChart`, `releaseCharts`, `afterEvent`, `waitForResize` and `triggerMouseEvent` ([Chart.js ADR 0022](adr/0022-chart-test-harness.md) sections 4 to 6), and the fixture runner of `lib/src/fixture.dart`: `specsFromFixtures`, `specFromFixture`, `FixtureSource`, `FixtureItem`, `FixtureInputs`, `FixtureLoad`, `HandFixture`, `fixturesTag`, `noSubmoduleReason` and `fixturesRequiredVariable` ([Chart.js ADR 0029](adr/0029-fixture-smoke-runner.md)) | `packages/chartjs_test_utils/test/fonts_test.dart`, `packages/chartjs_test_utils/test/dom_test.dart`, `packages/chartjs_test_utils/test/utils_test.dart`, `packages/chartjs_test_utils/test/index_test.dart`, `packages/chartjs_test_utils/test/fixture_test.dart` |
| `packages/chartjs_test_utils/lib/src/fonts.dart` | The test font (Chart.js ADR 0015). `installTestFont()` registers Liberation Sans 2.1.5 (regular, bold, italic, bold italic) with `FontLoader` under `Helvetica Neue`, `Helvetica`, `Arial`, `Liberation Sans` and `sans-serif`, once per isolate. It finds `lib/fonts/` through `.dart_tool/package_config.json`, because `Isolate.resolvePackageUri` is unsupported in the flutter tester | `packages/chartjs_test_utils/test/fonts_test.dart`: 'Hello' at 12px is 4667/2048*12 (regular) and 5007/2048*12 (bold) for each family name, the 'Wo' kerning applies, and a style with no family keeps the FlutterTest font |
| `packages/chartjs_test_utils/lib/src/dom.dart` | The DOM stand-in of the chart harness ([Chart.js ADR 0022](adr/0022-chart-test-harness.md) sections 3, 7 and 8). Not a pure layer. `document` (`body`, `createElement` for `div` and `canvas`, `getElementById`) and `window` (`devicePixelRatio`, which keeps the logical size of the test view). A canvas of `createElement` has a standby host: a `RenderChartHost` that is never attached. Thus a chart on it gets `FlutterPlatform` and starts detached. The end of the test disposes the standby hosts. A canvas that leaves the shown tree gets its standby host back after the frame. `HarnessElement` (`style`, `parentNode`, `childNodes`, `clientWidth`, `clientHeight`, `appendChild`, `removeChild`, `contains`, `remove`, `setAttribute`, `getAttribute`, `removeAttribute`), `CanvasParentElement` (`parentElement`, `remove` of a canvas) and `CssProperties` (`width`, `height`, `maxWidth`, `maxHeight`, `padding`, `display`, `position`, `direction`, `cssText`). The harness body: the first DOM write of a `testWidgets` body attaches `document.body` as the root widget. Each element is one render box with the CSS subset of the ADR: the px `width` and `height`, the content width of the parent, shrink-to-fit for `position: absolute` and the class `chartjs-wrapper`, the content height (the display size of a canvas), the px `max-width` and `max-height`, one px `padding`, `display: none` (size 0, the host stays mounted) and `direction: rtl`. A canvas child is a `ChartHost` with loose constraints of the content box. The body is 800 x 600 and places each child at its top left corner. A DOM write changes the tree at once and rebuilds the body. During build, layout or paint and in a forced layout, the rebuild waits for a post-frame callback. An append or a removal schedules the mutation checkpoint: one microtask with the forced build and layout, then `flushCanvasMutations`. A read of `clientWidth` or `clientHeight` does the forced layout first. After the unmount of the body, a write changes only the tree. The tree stays between the tests, as the DOM stays between the specs upstream. `forceHarnessLayout` (for `acquireChart`), `afterEachChart` (the unmount of the body calls each function), `setTimeout` (a harness task on `clock.now()`, cleared at the end of the test), `pumpFrames` (pumps of 16 ms, the due tasks between the pumps, the Jasmine timeout message after 5000 ms; one loop pumps at a time, and a loop that starts during another loop awaits each step of that loop), `waitForDone` and `pumpFrame` (the due tasks and one pump: the frame of the fixture runner, row `harness-fixture-frame`) | `packages/chartjs_test_utils/test/dom_test.dart` |

See [chartjs-test-utils 0.4.0](#chartjs-test-utils-040) | `packages/chartjs_test_utils/test/context_test.dart`, `packages/chartjs_test_utils/test/matchers_test.dart`, `packages/chartjs_test_utils/test/utils_test.dart`, `packages/chartjs_test_utils/test/index_test.dart` |

| `lib/src/theme/compile.dart` | The theme compiler of [Chart.js ADR 0050](adr/0050-public-theme-api.md) over raw Map themes, a pure layer ([theming.md](theming.md) §3.3). `compileChartTheme` compiles into a frozen typed `ChartOptionsScope` with the key shape of `Chart.defaults` ([Chart.js ADR 0054](adr/0054-typed-internal-model.md) §3): each object under a key of a nested kind is a frozen typed scope of that kind, an object under a key with no kind a frozen Map. A theme without keys gives `null`. A raw Map theme has the fields `colors`, `typography`, `palette` and `raw`. The public `ChartThemeData` of `theme_data.dart` converts to a raw Map theme without `raw` ([Chart.js ADR 0050](adr/0050-public-theme-api.md)). Thus in 0.1.0 no public input reaches the guard: the token emission is the allowlist, and the `raw` field is for the tests only. A color token is a `RawThemeColor`: the sRGB components of `Color.from`. `themeColorToRgba` gives its `rgba()` string with 8-bit channels and the alpha of `jsNumberToString`. The tokens emit the root `color`, the `scale` keys, the `scales.radialLinear` keys, the element keys, `plugins.title.color` and `plugins.subtitle.color` of theming.md §4. The tokens `inverseSurface` and `onInverseSurface` emit the tooltip `backgroundColor` and the tooltip `titleColor`, `bodyColor`, `footerColor` and `multiKeyBackground` (`plugin.tooltip.js:1279, 1280, 1287, 1292, 1305`). The point-label font is the `typography.font` Map without `size`, then the fields of `typography.pointLabels`. `plugins.title.font` and `plugins.subtitle.font` are the `typography.font` Map without `weight` (§3.4, `plugin.title.js:148-150`, `plugin.subtitle.js:35-37`). `plugins.legend.labels.font` and `plugins.legend.title.font` are the whole `typography.font` Map: the legend has no font defaults (`plugin.legend.js:636-712`), and `toFont` fills an absent field from `defaults.font`. `plugins.tooltip.bodyFont` is the whole `typography.font` Map, and `plugins.tooltip.titleFont` and `plugins.tooltip.footerFont` are the Map without `weight` (`plugin.tooltip.js:1281-1283, 1289-1290, 1295-1297`). The tooltip keys come after the legend keys, so the `plugins` Map has one order in each theme. The `palette` field (`colors`, `backgroundAlpha`) emits `plugins.colors.$borderColors` (the colors at their alpha) and `plugins.colors.$backgroundColors` (the colors at `backgroundAlpha`, 0.5 when absent, as `plugin.colors.ts:25`) ([Chart.js ADR 0050](adr/0050-public-theme-api.md)). The `raw` keys beat the token keys, key by key. `themeNeverEmittedReason` is the emission guard, the never-emitted list of §3.3, with rule 2b (a path of `themePluginBehaviorPaths`: the decimation, filler, legend and tooltip behavior keys and the title, subtitle and legend title texts) and rule 9 (a palette key with a value that is not a List with at least one item) of [Chart.js ADR 0050](adr/0050-public-theme-api.md). The compiler leaves out each key that the guard rejects. In debug mode, an assert then fails. The `transitions` keys come only beside other keys. `jsObjectFreeze` freezes each scope, each Map and each List of the result. A write into the result throws a `TypeError`. No memo: each call compiles ([Chart.js ADR 0054](adr/0054-typed-internal-model.md) §4). Ported files never import the file. | `test/port/widgets/chart_theme_switch_test.dart` | [theming.md](theming.md) §8 test 8, the theme switch of `ChartView` ([Chart.js ADR 0050](adr/0050-public-theme-api.md), VM only). The group 'the forms of the probe F-P4': F0 (no reset: the old ticks and palette stay), F1 (a copy of the options that the chart changed: the old ticks stay, the options lose their identity) and F3 (a pristine copy: the title edit and the identity go) on charts of `acquireChart`, and F2, the widget: the new ticks, the title edit stays, `chart.config.options` keeps its identity, and the edit of `scales.x.min` goes (the documented limit). 'a switch': the element, scale and palette colors of bar, line and radar charts (also the point-label and angle-line colors of the radar); a hidden dataset stays hidden, and the controllers, the data arrays and the dataset objects stay; the decimated data stay (`min-max` and `lttb`); a removal gives the upstream colors with `update('default')`; `'theme'` animates a bar color for 200 ms `'linear'` (at 100 ms the colors interpolator at 0.5, at 200 ms the end color); an equal theme and `const ChartThemeData()` against no theme give no `beforeUpdate`; a dataset that the app pushes after the first colorize gets the next palette color after a switch; an app edit of a dataset color stays, and the other datasets then get no palette color, as a new chart with that dataset color; `forceOverride` keeps the value before the first write; a theme change and a config change in one frame give one update with the theme mode; the inherited `ChartTheme`, `ChartView.theme` beats it, and `const ChartThemeData()` turns theming off. 'the widget': the plugin `$chartView` comes last, the diagnostics name the theme, and the debug asserts of an app `$theme` and of a `plugins` value that is not a List. 'reduced motion': `ChartThemeSwitch.apply` gives `'themeFrame'` and `'default'`, and an `update('themeFrame')` ends in one frame; under `MediaQuery(disableAnimations: true)`, the widget updates with `'themeFrame'` for a switch and `'default'` for a removal, and both end in one frame (the flag of [Chart.js ADR 0049](adr/0049-chart-view-widget.md) §7) | — |
