The pinned runner is the CI job `fixtures` on `ubuntu-24.04`; only a `workflow_dispatch` run with `update_goldens` writes the files (the artifact `theme-goldens`). The golden files came from the dispatch run 41001061748 at `6c461f3`; each other run of the job compares them | — |
| `lib/src/widgets/chart_keyboard.dart` | `ChartKeyboard` (`@internal`), the keyboard of `ChartView` ([Chart.js ADR 0051](adr/0051-input-events-and-interaction-state.md) sections 2 to 7). The targets of each key: the legend group (the centers of `legendHitBoxes`, read through `types.LegendElement`, when the legend shows; a hit box of the legend plugin reads through the port-only interface `types.TypedLegendHitBox`, another object by name, [Chart.js ADR 0054](adr/0054-typed-internal-model.md) §12), then a group for each visible dataset in index order (the final center `getCenterPoint(true)` of each element with a finite center and a visible data index). The target is a group and an index: an index past the end takes the last item, and a group that is gone clears the target. The key table of §3 for `KeyDownEvent` and `KeyRepeatEvent` (Right, Left, Down, Up, Home, End, Enter, numpad Enter, Space, Escape); a `KeyUpEvent` and a key with Shift, Control, Alt or Meta give `ignored`. A move sends a `mousemove` `NativeEvent` at the target point through `dispatchCanvasEvent`, with the `KeyEvent` as `source` (the virtual mouse of §4); Enter and Space send `mousedown`, `mouseup` and `click`, once for a key down; Escape and a focus loss send `mouseout` at (-1, -1), outside the canvas, as a real mouse that leaves it, and clear the target. Each key event first goes to the canvas listeners as `keydown` (and `keypress` for an event with a character) or `keyup`, with NaN offsets, only when the canvas has a listener for the type (§5). The focus ring of §6, from the `afterDraw` of the widget plugin: a 2 px `strokeRect` 2 px outside the hit box of a legend target or 1 px inside the canvas box with no target, in the resolved `chart.options.color`, only with the focus and the traditional highlight mode; a change of the focus, of the highlight mode or of the ring rectangle calls `chart.render()`. It imports no plugin. Not a pure layer | `test/port/widgets/chart_keyboard_test.dart` |
| `test/port/widgets/chart_keyboard_test.dart` | The keyboard of `ChartView` ([Chart.js ADR 0051](adr/0051-input-events-and-interaction-state.md), VM only). The oracle of a key move is `triggerMouseEvent` at the same point on a second `ChartView` of the same config and size. 'the keys give the state of a mouse at the target': for a bar, a line, a doughnut and a radar chart, each move of Down, Right, Left, End, Home and Up over the legend items and the elements of 2 datasets, and Escape, give the active elements, the tooltip items and the legend `onHover` and `onLeave` log of the mouse; the mode `'index'` gives both datasets. 'click': Enter and Space on legend item 1 hide and show its dataset, and Down then skips a hidden dataset; the doughnut toggles the data index; a custom legend `onClick` gets `legendItems[1]`; Enter on an element calls `options.onClick` with the elements of a mouse click there; a repeat of Enter gives no second click. 'options.events': `events: ['click']` gives no active element on a move, and Enter still clicks; `keydown`, `keypress` (a character key) and `keyup` reach an inline `beforeEvent` with NaN `x` and `y`, the `keydown` before the `mousemove` of its key; without them, no key event and no key listener. 'Escape, the focus loss and the ignored keys': Escape and the focus loss clear the hover and the tooltip, with the oracle `mouseout` at (-1, -1); Escape and the focus loss on a legend item call the legend `onLeave`; with no target, Left, Up, Home, End, Enter, Space and Escape give `ignored`; a key with Shift gives `ignored`; Escape with no target reaches an ancestor `DismissIntent` action of a `WidgetsApp`; at the last target, Right moves the focus to the next focusable; a new chart (a new `platform`) has no target. 'the focus stop': Tab stops at the chart; `ExcludeFocus` and a `FocusNode(canRequestFocus: false)` give no stop; the diagnostics name `focusNode` and `autofocus`; `autofocus` focuses the chart at its first build; a `setState` in `onHover` of a key move and in `onComplete` of the `render()` of a focus change throws nothing. 'the focus ring': the draw calls (`drawCalls` of `scale_chart_harness.dart`) end with the ring at the chart box and at a legend target, only with the focus and the traditional highlight mode, and with none at a data target; a focus change, a new ring rectangle and a new highlight mode call `render()`; the ring takes the resolved `options.color`, and `plugins.$chartView: false` turns it off | — |
